# LANE: the weights-based streaming tracker (goal-class)

Opened 2026-09-01. Graph node: P2R0G19B_PHYSICAL_G3_TRACKER.
Parent trail: LANE_h2_h3_endpoint_adjoint.md (the full derivation
campaign; every component below carries a certificate there).

## The problem, exactly

Raw <1e-8 needs per-layer variance supply at corr ~0.8+ vs the true
residual field. Proven: the card's carried pair-state is
INFORMATION-LIMITED (exact consumption == its series) — 0.8-class
supply cannot come from carried planes; identification from them is
capped at the measured ~0.2 parity. The WEIGHTS contain everything:
the chaos frame is exact and identification-free through L2 (C at
banked-truth level within sampling noise), and the per-unit engines
are certified at 0.1% (trapezoid law). The single unsolved piece is
DEPTH-TRANSPORT: propagating the chaos state past L2 without the
error compounding that killed the naive plane recursion (14x worse
than the card by L14).

## Assets (all certified, receipts in the parent lane)

- Resolvent-exact emission: E[phi''' d3], E[phi'' d], alpha/B/T per
  unit — no ladder; beta 0.997-0.9994 at 1024 spectra.
- Certified Wick assemblies: K21post (1,1,1,1/2,1); K22/K31 (a,M)
  formulas (1,2,2,2,8 / 3,6,6); kappa3/kappa4 Jordan-Gram identities.
- Shared-basis carry economics: H3/H4 sectors concentrate to shared
  rank r90 ~15->6 with depth; shared-basis transport exactly
  mixing-closed; honest carry R~n/2 (tiny world).
- The three numerics laws (CF denominator; trapezoid endpoints;
  CF-only moment identities) with certificates.
- Targets: R-fields both nets at 1e-5 shared-draw truth (per layer);
  mps_planes truth pre-K21/K22/K31 l>=2; the consumption oracle.
- The anchored-delta and difference-first laws: never rebuild the
  card; compute residual supplies as differences of matched models.

## The build shape (v1 prototype)

State per unit c at layer l: (m_c, a_c in R^1024, M_c FACTORED).
L1 exact: a = al1*(Wsig ahat); M = Ahat^T diag(u) Ahat; exact-var
Gaussian-tail completion (certified).
Step l -> l+1:
  1. Per-unit engines (top-K eigenmodes + tail mode): resolvent
     post-state (alpha_c, B_c, T_c) — the certified path.
  2. Mix EXACTLY (linear in coefficients): a' = W^T alpha (dense);
     M' = W^T-conjugated B in FACTORED form — the representation
     decision (the open research core):
       v1a: M' carried as B-words in the running dictionary
            {ahat x ahat} + spillover tracked;
       v1b: M' re-factored per unit by truncated eigh of the mixed
            operator (top-K + tail; K~64-128);
       v1c: shared-basis (V-frame) with per-unit small cores
            (S_c = V^T M_c V), V from the layer's a-span + M-energy
            operator, refreshed per layer.
  3. H3 sector: rank-R shared basis, birth from the certified words,
     transport theta' = W^T theta (exactly closed).
  4. Readout at each layer: pair field C/K21 via the Gram assemblies;
     SUPPLY = difference vs the closure on matched implied stats
     (the difference-first law); grade vs banked R-fields.

## Preregistered gates

- T1: the prototype reaches L5 on rank35 with pair-field supply
  corr >= 0.6 vs the banked R-field (pushed-v metric). Context: the
  plane-identified route's ceiling is ~0.2; the exact-shallow frame
  at L2 is ~1.0 by construction — T1 asks that depth-transport
  preserve most of the exactness for three more layers.
- T2: frozen replication on rank100: same-sign, magnitude within 30%.
- T3: consumption value: only_v >= +8% raw on both nets from the
  tracker-supplied layers (vs the oracle's ~+47-53% full-channel).
- Any gate fail: bank the wall with its measured location (which
  layer, which sector, what error) and re-derive; the fifteen-plus
  fences say walls here are usually representation choices, not
  physics.

## Standing discipline

Machine: ONE job, nice 15, OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=4; MPS one at a
time. Every derived formula gets a certificate BEFORE use (three
catches law). Single-net numbers are mirages: rank35 develop,
rank100 frozen, panel nets untouched until the belief ladder.
Bank every cell in this file as built.

dictionary transport, L2 carried C **7.4%** (card: 0.6%). The
mixing algebra is exactly closed and GEMM-class (2 dicts at L2).
The one-step loss is the DROPPED SPILLOVER: B's sym(a,Ma)-class
words and the T-sector. EXTENSION (next cell): dictionary-PAIR
weights — M_c = sum_{d,d'} D_d^T diag(w_c^{dd'}) D_{d'}; the
(Ma)-vectors join as their own dictionary; cross-terms are diagonal
in the unit index; Grams between dictionary pairs keep everything
GEMM-class; growth 2-3 dicts/layer (fine to L16). Then re-measure
the one-step fidelity; iterate toward gate T1 (L5, supply corr
>=0.6 vs banked R). Package track in parallel: L6 gate landed at
the parity band (0.1775) — fifth layer at the same level; its solo
consumption value queues with the L3/L7 fills.

T-4 DESIGN (the anchored synthesis): the deployable form re-anchors
on the card's carried planes EVERY layer (no compounding, per the

EXTRAS the card provably lacks (the >=3 cross-information, T-sector,

— the G3-engine-delta at parity 0.18 is this WITHOUT extras; the
tracked extras are the lever above parity. T1-gate trajectory: the
unanchored carry reaches L5 at ~13% (6x short); the anchored form
needs only one-step fidelity in the card's error direction.

## T-4 v1 (2026-09-01): NULL below parity — the assimilation step was the design, and v1 skipped it

Fast-path validated first: the 30x engines reproduce T-3 exactly
(L2 0.01642/L3 0.05781; K=128 mode-compression certificate 1.5e-3;
short t-grid + analytic tail; full trajectory L4 10.4% / L5 15.0%,
~4.5%/step). T-4 v1 (tracked-state difference-first supply, diag-
anchored only): corr 0.029 (L5) / 0.016 (L4) vs fitted-core parity
0.18 — the drifted PAIR structure swamps the supply; local refits
beat exact-rooted-but-drifted state at the layers they are read.
REDESIGN (T-5, the actual anchored architecture): CARD-ASSIMILATED
TRACKING — at each layer, project the tracked state onto consistency
with the card's carried planes (deploy-available; one Gauss-Newton
step in the dictionary weights toward C_card; fits only to the card,
never truth — deploy-legal), keep the extras (Ttail, C_extra,
alpha-structure), transport, repeat. The card carries the bulk at
0.6-1.2%; the tracker carries only what the card lacks. Supply =
[my model on the assimilated state] - [closure(card stats)].
Cadence note: each cycle now ~30-60 min (fast engines).

## Q4 emission CERTIFIED (2026-09-01): the fourth exact resolvent formula

E[grad^4 phi] = E[phi'''' d4] + 6 sym(E[phi''' d2] (x) M)
              + 3 sym(E[phi''] M (x) M), with tilted moments
E[d4 e^{itz}] = CF[m4 + 6sym(mmC) + 3sym(CC)], E[d2 e^{itz}] =
CF[mm' + C], kernels (it)^2 / it / p(0). MC certificate (r=5,
N=6e6, six index patterns): corr 0.99991; ratios 0.998/1.039/0.995/
0.999/0.987 on the material entries (the (0,1,2,3) entry 1.12 is
noise-dominated at 2e-3 magnitude). The Q4 channel is buildable
exactly; its word/exact form choice waits on the T-Gram probe
(word-vs-exact fidelity at depth), running.

## T-14 (2026-09-01) `_rk*`/`_blk*`/`_perm*` are TANGENT plane caps (DK21_RANK/BLOCK/PERM on the injected dK21 plane), not carrier caps
run_tangent_v2.py:301-306. rk256: corr 0.79-0.83 at L11-15 (= full) but β drifts 1.11→0.30 with depth; low-β sweep peaks +4.9 (β 0.15); per-layer rms-matched to base peaks +4.2 (β 0.3) vs full +10.3 → the injected plane is genuinely high-rank (consistent with dk21-value-identity-specific). Irrelevant to deploy cost: a 1024² plane per layer is GEMM-class. The deploy question is the ENGINE that emits it (1024³ carried cum3 tensor, push 3×1024⁴/layer ≈ 42× the 2^41 budget over 14 layers). Decisive test = TUCKER_R truncation of the carried tensor inside the engine (exact under the ReLU g⊗g⊗g scaling and the W push; deploy cost R×1024² per mode): if Tucker-256 keeps ~+10% → deployable route; if it collapses → the +10-15% is an oracle ceiling and the fire path returns to a compressed carrier (tracker class). Deployed card already carries a CP3 state (_CP3_CAP 16384, Winograd arena) + rank-2048 CP4 — the deploy-frame engine's supply is measured NET of that carrier.

## T-15 (2026-09-01) DEPLOY WALL DIAGNOSED: the kt-family collapse is the ENGINE's planes (cancellation broken), and Tucker-256 keeps the planes sane but not the value → flat spectrum
Per-layer pre-plane gap to host (`tensor_planes_*` pre_L vs host_cov k21), r35:
| L | truth−host rms | base dK rms (corr to truth gap) | kt dK rms (corr) | dep dK rms (corr) |
| 2 | 5.70e-4 | 1.80e-4 (0.310) | 2.55e-3 (−0.148) | 1.77e-4 (0.304) |
| 5 | 5.27e-4 | 4.42e-4 (0.773) | 2.45e-3 (0.244) | 4.40e-4 (0.772) |
| 11 | 4.04e-4 | 3.72e-4 (0.787) | 1.44e-3 (0.387) | 3.67e-4 (0.791) |
The KILL_TENSOR wedge (zero distinct content, re-seed planes) yields pre-planes 4.5× off-scale from L2 with negative corr: the pushed plane is a cancellation between plane-slice terms and distinct entries; removing the distinct content breaks it (same family as the delta-frame law: standalone plane recursion 14× worse). Not a tangent/consumption bug.
Tucker-256 (`TUCKER_B=256`, HOSVD of the distinct content, planes preserved) keeps the cancellation: plane rel vs MC truth 0.271/0.352/0.350/0.335 (corr 0.96/0.94/0.94/0.94) at L1-4, vs FULL tensor 0.117/0.082/0.070/0.066 (corr 0.993-0.998) and NO carried content (kt r100) 0.375 at L5. Rank 256 of 1024 buys ~0.04 of the 0.30 plane-error gap → the distinct content's spectrum is FLAT (unsummarizable; phase-1 cap-ladder-loses-once-billed, dk21-value-identity-specific re-confirmed at 1024×16). The TUCKER_B=128 leg now prints the mode-Gram energy fractions at r64/128/256/512 per layer (edit at run_tensor_recursion_v4.py:892+). Value verdict (tangent → consumption at β 0.4) pending in `optbeta_sweep_all_variants.log`.
Consequence: the deploy-frame +10.6/+15.3% is an ORACLE CEILING of the engine form (1024⁴/layer). The deployable carrier must compress the DIFFERENCE from the closure's own model (difference-first law) or be a different object (tracker class). Tracker standalone today: +0.8% at β 0.2.

The tracker's current information does not convert through the L16 transport: deployable value ≈ 0.
- Plain Monte Carlo at the full budget (N = 2^41 / (16·2·1024²) = 65,536 forward samples; target = L16 post-mean, mean post-var 6.73e-2): raw MSE 1.03e-6 = 35× the closure card (2.94e-8), 158× the leader's adj. A control-variate hybrid would need >150× variance reduction just to tie the leader → sampling routes stay dead at 1024×16.
- The card's own third-order carrier is a CP-16384 full-birth state (`_CP3_CAP`); the full-tensor engine beats it by 10-15% of MSE at β 0.40. The deployable question is therefore NOT "compress T_full" (flat spectrum, T-15) but "is (T_full − T_card) compressible?" — the difference-first law at the tensor level. Needs the card's CP3 factors dumped per layer for rank35 (extend the host tap in run_attribution.py / a CP3 dump from estimator_cap.py's state), then HOSVD energy fractions of the difference vs of T_full. If the difference is low-Tucker-rank (≤ ~32 per mode) a deployable increment carrier exists at ~1024²·R·3·14 flops. NEXT BLOCK.

## T-18 (2026-09-01) Tucker-256 VALUE collapses (−87% at β 0.4) despite sane planes → the carrier criterion is "plane error ≲ the closure's own 7%"; frozen β_v=0.40 positive on 3/3 nets
- `_tb256` through tangent → consumption (r35): only_v −471/−151/−87/−40/−9.5 at β 0.82/0.5/0.4/0.3/0.2. dv̂ per layer: rms 0.9-1.2e-3 (truth 4e-4), corr 0.01→0.53 (L3→L15), β 0.00→0.24 — the kt/aux signature, even though the engine's planes read rel 0.26-0.35 / corr 0.94-0.97 vs MC truth.

A 30%-accurate carrier injects 4× the gap as noise. Criterion for ANY deployable carrier: per-layer plane relative error ≲ 7% (none carried: 37%; Tucker-256: 30%; card's CP-16384: ~7%; full tensor: ~7%).
- Consequence for the GOAL: even the exact third-order tensor at bond order 6 is not more accurate than the closure at the plane level (K22/K31 carried_rel 0.17-0.24 in the same diag → fourth-order-and-up dominate the 7%). Raw < 1e-8 (−66%) needs plane errors ~1-2% → exact fourth-order-plus carriers (1024⁵-class). Not a budget question any more; cumulant carriers of any order cannot reach the goal at 1024×16 in 2^41.

## T-21 (2026-09-01) THE CHEAP COMPRESSION OF THE WHOLE THIRD ORDER, re-found ([instruction removed]): the C1 endpoint adjoint (08-31) — and tonight's reframing supplies what it lacked
- LANE_h2_h3 §1 + C1 (receipt `public_g19_attribution_v1/C1_ADJOINT_RANK_rank0035.json`, `run_c1_adjoint_rank.py`): the final-mean effect of the ENTIRE third-order plane error is S = Σ_l ⟨dK21_l, M_l⟩ with the closed-form t0 adjoint M_l = diag(a2_l)[Σ_{L>l} B_{l→L} diag(S_v·g_L) B_{l→L}ᵀ] diag(e1_l), B = products of diag(e1_j)W_{j+1} — all card-computable. Sanity: Σ_l ⟨dK21_l, M_l⟩ = 15.7% linear capture, matching the certified chain. VALUE-RANK of M_l (reads needed for 95% of the value): L7 104, L8 135, L9 >200 (exception), L10 45, L11 14, L12 93, L13 11, L14 8. ⇒ the whole third-order effect on the answer is ~10-150 bilinear scalar reads x_rᵀ dK21_l y_r per layer, with KNOWN read directions.
- Why it was shelved (C3 FAIL 08-31): each read is a partial-diagonal contraction of the cum3 tensor; without the tensor the only known supply was randomized sign-draw projections, which needed 10³-10⁴ draws per read (off/diag mass ratio ~45). C4v1 closed form: NULL (the raw birth content cancels in engine − host).
- What tonight changes (T-19): dK21_l does NOT need the tensor. The value rides order-2 content the card already carries (CP3 births), and the engine−card difference is the plane EMISSION (1024²-class) on the card's own dense planes (+ optionally the CP-1024/layer delta carry, whose plane read is ≤5% util in total). So the reads become x_rᵀ E_l y_r with E_l = emission_engine(card state) − emission_card(card state): dense 1024² per layer, 10-150 bilinear forms per layer ≈ 1e8-1e9 flops/layer — negligible. This is SUB-2 in its final, derived, post-hoc form: card unchanged (no coadaptation), no learned payload, whole third-order effect compressed to ~1e3 scalars.
- Build order: (1) SUB-1 (teammate A) decides whether the emission delta E_l reproduces the engine's value on the card's state (retrofit or post-hoc); (2) if yes, the deploy form = adjoint reads of E_l (this entry) + CP delta carry only if the carried share (FRESH_STOP=8 kept 0.42 of value) is needed; (3) certify Σ_r ⟨E_l, x_r y_rᵀ⟩ against the dense ⟨E_l, M_l⟩ per layer (instrument, minutes); (4) panel LOO β, virgin gate, fire.

## T-20 (2026-09-01, teammate B "supply-ladder", PART 1) — the v-supply quality ladder on r35 (deploy frame; consumption_betas.py; board raw 2.9409e-8, instrument baseline 2.676e-8; exact v = −54%)
Frames (run_tensor_recursion_v4.py:299-319): BOTH frames self-supply k3u (diag of the carried pushed plane) and k4u/K22/K31 (engine's own fourth push); DEFAULT feeds truth m,v,C; DEPLOY feeds host m,v,C. `_dep` ≈ base (10.6 vs 10.3) → m,v,C provenance is worth ~0. All arms below run ON TOP OF DEPLOY_FRAME=1 with exactly one input made exact (engine copy `run_tensor_recursion_v4_oracle.py`: additive suffixes, ORACLE_K3U, per-suffix receipts `TENSOR_RECURSION_rank0035<sfx>_oracle.json`; driver `run_ladder_oracle.sh`, log `ladder_oracle_r35.log`, fine sweeps `ladder_oracle_fine_k34.log`).
| arm (r35) | only_v at opt β (β) | only_k3 at opt (BK3) | only_k4 at opt (BK4) | joint at per-arm optima | engine K21 rel err L13 (closure 0.129) |
| `_dep` current rung | **+10.6 (0.40)** | +2.1 (0.20) | +0.6 (0.10) | +14.4 | 0.131 |
| `_dep_o4` truth k4u + K22/K31 | +7.4 (0.30) | +3.7 (0.20) | +5.7 (0.25) | +18.7 | 0.084 |
| `_dep_ok3` truth univariate k3 | +7.9 (0.30) | +6.8 (0.20) | +0.6 (0.10) | +17.0 | — |
| `_dep_opl` truth PRE K21 plane fed each layer (one-step-ahead) | +7.8 (0.30) | +6.6 (0.33) | 0.0 | — | 0.071 (one-step) |
| `_dep_o4_ok3_opl` all three | (pending, PART 2) | | | | |
| `_true` = TRUTH K21 pre-planes through the SAME tangent (tangent mode trueK21) | **+34.8 (0.50)** (+23.4 at β 1.0) | — | — | — | 0 |
| `_true_pairs` = truth K21+K22+K31 through the tangent (TANGENT_PAIRS=1) | **+35.0 (0.70)** | — | — | — | 0 |
READINGS
1. Making any single ENGINE INPUT exact LOWERS the v-arm (10.6 → 7.4/7.9/7.8) while raising that input's own arm (truth k3 → +6.8, truth k4 → +5.7). Exactness of inputs is NOT the binding rung. The engine's plane gets closer to MC truth (rel 0.131 → 0.084 / 0.071) yet the dv supply does not improve — plane rel error and dv fidelity are only weakly related.
2. A PERFECT third-order pre-plane through the existing tangent+consumption is worth **+34.8%** (per-layer dv corr 0.83-0.99, β≈1.0); adding perfect fourth-order planes adds nothing (+35.0). So the third-order plane channel's ceiling through this consumption path is ~35% of MSE (board 2.94e-8 → ~1.9e-8); the remaining ~19 points of the exact-v pool (54%) are NOT reachable by plane injections (closure form / other channels).
3. The one-step plane map (ReLU map + emission + push) is the binding element: fed the TRUTH pre-plane at every layer, its predicted next pre-plane yields dv corr 0.18-0.86 by layer — the same as the fully carried engine (0.19-0.84) — vs 0.83-0.99 for the truth plane itself. One-step rel error 0.05-0.07 vs truth ≈ the whole (truth − host) signal (~7% of the plane).
4. Transport is NOT the loss: per-layer transported fidelity corr(T dv̂_l, T truth_l) = 0.60-0.76 (raw 0.50-0.84); transport gains equal for dv̂ and truth (‖T x‖/‖x‖ 0.055/0.060 at L5 … 0.168/0.169 at L15); collective (mean-direction) component of dv̂ negligible (coll frac ≤0.005; `_dep_perp` = +10.6 = full; `_dep_colldv` = 0.0). Value ≈ corr² × pool: 0.7² × ~23-35 ≈ 10.6 ✓.
5. Per-layer value maps (only_v, isolation): `_dep` at β0.4: L10-15 = 1.5/2.3/1.1/2.0/2.8/1.9, L7 = −2.3, L4 = −0.7, L2-6 ≈ 0 (sum 9.6 ≈ joint 10.6). `_true` at β0.5: L8-15 = 4.0/2.8/2.0/3.9/6.2/5.7/4.0/7.5, L5 = −1.7 (sum ≈ 35). Deep layers carry the value; the third-order pool lives at L8-15.
6. Per-layer β vectors do NOT transfer: 14 free betas in-sample 21-30% but LOO (fit on two of 35/100/150, test on third) 10.2/0.0/3.6; quadratic-in-depth 11.8/7.4/8.6 vs single β 10.6/15.1/7.6; dropping L4+L7 helps r35 only (+2.8) — net-specific. Single frozen β_v = 0.40 stands.
7. k3/k4 arms of the current supply are worthless (best +2.1/+0.6); with truth-fed inputs they become +6.8/+5.7 (the truth arms), and joint reaches +17-19 — but those arms then require the truth inputs.
CONSEQUENCE for the climb: raise the ONE-STEP plane map's dv-relevant fidelity (corr 0.7 → 0.9 ≈ +28%, → 0.95 ≈ +32% of the 35-pt ceiling), not the inputs, not the transport, not per-layer betas. Which component of the one-step map — the emission formula, the carried distinct content, or the push — is measured in PART 2 (ablations `_dep_gateonly`, `_dep_cardplane`, and the truth-POST-plane wedge `_dep_opost`).

Where the remaining points are lost (B's fact): the engine's post-ReLU plane is 2-6% off vs truth, the next pre-plane 7-14% — the push is exact, so the DISTINCT (off-plane) entries carry the larger error and inject it into every next plane. Running wedges decide the mechanism: all-inputs-exact arm (does exactness of every input lift the v-arm at all? if not, the engine FORM caps the path), truth-post-plane wedge (perfect plane slices + engine distinct content: if the next pre-plane is still 7-14% off, the distinct-content map is the error source), gate-only and card-plane ablations (which term carries today's 10.6).
If the distinct-content map is the source, the next derived mechanism is its generation formula (candidate on record: the coincidence-resummed generation, phase-1 unit-bond-coincidence-law: 10.4% → 0.82% third-order error at L2, derived, no fit; in a dense engine it is a formula change, not a rank cost). Rule for the next 3 hours: no packaging of a 10% rung; the chase is the fidelity mechanism, with the sidecar as its carrier.

## T-28 (2026-09-01) Teammate A FINAL (public_g20_emission_retrofit_v1/LANE.md §1-12): the mechanism is the CARRY of the pushed residual's distinct entries, not the emission; all sidecar forms priced

Term diff: same nine first-order Edgeworth channels, same connected formula; card has 7 extra L3² channels but 7 Mehler terms vs the engine's 20; no engine input missing in the card. ⇒ N1.2a CLOSED (null), N1.2g-direct (emission-delta sidecar) DEAD (−0.1…−1.3% r35, 0.0% r100).
- THE mechanism: the card stores R = k21_target − CP-plane as a star pushed exactly ONCE (`_push_cubic` :1134, certified = CP form at 5e-16) and DROPS the distinct entries that push generates; the engine keeps them forever. Carrying them in-host: host (F2J-off) −7.9% r35 / −5.0% r100 (window L1-6, sign-stable), planes move halfway to the engine's at L3-8; deep windows harmful; F2J-on +3.9% / +18.1% (the learned source undoes it); half strength −0.67% full card; top-256/512 rows +162/+95% (the residual is a COHERENT object, cannot be row-thinned); bill +17% util (6144 terms). - Goal-class reading with B's all-oracle arm (every input exact → v-arm 7.5%, identical per-layer dv fidelity): exact THIRD-order carry caps the v-arm at ~10-15% in this engine form. The remaining ~35 of 47 variance points need content the third-order carrier does not have (fourth-order distinct content and/or the map's own form) — B's truth-post-plane wedge decides which. Derived candidate on record for the fourth order's endpoint effect: SOL's H4 reverse-adjoint theorem (LANE_h4_reverse_adjoint.md: every bare H4 square birth accumulates exactly into one O(n²) object with O(Ln³) work).

CEILING ROW (coordinator's request; the decisive number): TRUTH pre-planes (mps_planes K21_L − host_cov k21) injected as dK21 at every layer through run_tangent_v2 (mode trueK21) → consumption_betas (F2J-on deployed baseline 2.676e-8, r35): **only_v +34.8% at β 0.5** (+35.8 at 0.7; +23.4 at β 1.0); with truth K22/K31 planes also injected (TANGENT_PAIRS=1): +35.0. **Joint with the truth k3/k4 arms at their optima (BK3 0.33, BK4 0.25): +53.3%.** Instrument's own oracle (truth v,k3,k4 STATES): only_v 47.4 (48.0 at opt β 0.73), joint 73.5 (74.6 at opt). The goal (−66%) needs BOTH plane fidelity toward the 35-point ceiling AND a consumer beyond the linearized pair map (a second-order pair consumer) for the remaining 12 v / 20 joint points.
LADDER VERDICT: (1) supply fidelity is the first-order lever — the engine is at 10.6 of the 35-point plane ceiling because its per-layer dv corr is 0.5-0.85 (value ≈ corr² × pool); (2) no input's exactness raises that fidelity (all oracle arms 7.4-8.1, identical per-layer corr); the fidelity is set by the multi-push transport of the carried distinct entries — teammate A's finding (emission ≡ card read to 1e-5; the value is the distinct entries born by pushing the plane residual, which the card drops after one push) is confirmed from three sides here (opost: emission not the limiter; opl: perfect pre-plane in, same fidelity out; gateonly: the transport is mostly gate+push); (3) every oracle arm reads ~3 points BELOW `_dep` not from fidelity but from per-layer scale drift (their deep-layer β falls to 0.76-0.80 while `_dep`'s stays 0.83-0.93 flat; a single global β cannot serve a depth-drifting gain) — a per-layer β vector does not transfer across nets (T-20 part 1 §6), so flat scale is a property to preserve in any new carrier; (4) transport, collective mode, k3/k4 arms are not where the loss is.
NEXT RUNGS implied: (a) carry the plane-residual distinct entries in CP form (gate+push = 75% of the engine's value at the card's CP3 cost class) → +8-10% deployable; (b) raise the carried content's multi-push fidelity toward the truth plane (the 35-point ceiling: each +0.1 of dv corr ≈ +5-6 points); (c) the second-order pair consumer for the 12+20 points beyond the linearization.
Machine notes: three engines ran sequentially (never two 1024³ at once; free memory ≥ 72% throughout). ## T-33 (2026-09-01) Rank spectrum of the CARRIED RESIDUAL (teammate A's window-6 residual CP factors, r35; `public_g20_emission_retrofit_v1/residual_spectrum_r35.log`)
Mode-Gram Tucker energy fractions of the carried residual (CP terms λ p⊗p⊗q, gated+pushed; rank = 1024×min(l,6), decaying after L6 as terms age):
| layer (rank) | p-mode energy kept at r32 / r128 / r256 | q-mode at r32 / r128 |
| L3 (3072) | 0.13 / 0.38 / 0.58 | 0.54 / 0.89 |
| L6 (6144) | 0.22 / 0.52 / 0.71 | 0.73 / 0.96 |

Reading: the residual is NOT low-rank on the p-side at the early layers (rank 256 keeps 58-71% at L3-6) but concentrates with depth; the q-side is compressible everywhere. Crucially this is a DIFFERENCE object: a carrier that drops 30% of the residual's energy makes a plane error of ~0.3 × 7% ≈ 2% of the plane — inside the ≲7% carrier criterion (unlike Tucker-256 of the FULL tensor, which was 30% of the plane). Cost of a Tucker-(256,256,128) carrier of the residual: ~8e8 flops/layer push ≈ 0.5% util over 14 layers vs +17% for the raw 6144-term CP carry. ⇒ the deployable form of N1.2b is a rank-compressed residual carrier; expected value ~70-90% of the in-card window-6 gain (host −7.9% r35 / −5.0% r100) at ~1% util — a rung, pending the F2J refit (F2J-on today +3.9%) and the same-sample re-score. Instruments: estimator_retro.py RETRO_DUMP_BANK (ungated pushed factors + pre-state per layer; the dump run reproduces the deployed output to 1.2e-5, bankdump_r35.log), bond_kernel_oracle.py (batched R0/D1/D2 reads), cert_bond_kernel_small.py (dense n=24 certificate vs the engine's bond-0/1/2 kernel: rel err 1e-14, PASSED). r35 oracle running (oracle_r35.log); r100 bank dumped.
- T-68 RESULT (r35, BOND_KERNEL_rank0035.json, 131 s on MPS): the gate-only kernel is NOT the mechanism. The CP carrier's gate-only read is 75-90% of the truth k3 at every layer (rms 7-10e-3 vs truth 7.6-17e-3); the exact bond-1 correction to it is only 0.4-0.7% of that read (rms 1.1e-4 at L2 falling to 2.7e-5 at L15) = 3-19% of the residual dk3 (rms ~1e-3, FLAT in absolute terms across layers while the truth k3 decays 1.7e-2 -> 7.6e-3, which is why the relative error grows 3.5% -> 11%); bond-2 is 20x smaller still. Against dk3: corr 0.03-0.21, energy explained <= 4% (L2) and <= 1.7% at L8-15; beta 0.6-3 (noisy). CLOSED as a one-step mechanism (an accumulated version cannot exceed ~10x the one-step size; the residual's accumulation would also have to be coherent). Structural fact banked: the residual is ~1e-3 absolute at every layer, 30x the 32M truth's own noise at depth, 75-90% of k3 rides on the CP births. NEXT (T-69): the plane-to-distinct FEED (first order in the carried pre-plane; engine feed_factor with 6 orientations, coefficient 1/2, connected patterns tus+tws >= 1) - absent from the card; its next-layer per-unit read is GEMM-class for one-sided spectator bonds and 1024^4-class for two-sided; oracle plane_feed_oracle.py on the card's own pre-plane (RETRO_DUMP_BANK now also dumps plane_L{l}).
- T-69 (r35; PLANE_FEED_rank0035.json, PLANE_FEED_ACC_rank0035_onesided.json; cert_plane_feed_small.py PASSED 30 patterns at f32): the PLANE-TO-DISTINCT FEED IS A LIVE MECHANISM. One-step (feed born at layer l from the card's own pre-plane, read at l+1 on distinct entries only): rms 1.6-2.7e-4 = 16-27% of the residual dk3 at every layer, slope beta 0.9-1.15 (L2-L11; 1.05-1.48 at L12-15), corr 0.19-0.31, energy explained 4-10% one-step. ONE PATTERN carries it all: (tuw,tus,tws) = (0,1,0) = (1/2) K21pre[u,w] * (-alpha_u pdf_u/sd_u) * cdf_w * (sd_s cdf_s) * rho_us (spectator s tied to the doubled index u by ONE correlation bond); (0,0,1) is 4-6x smaller, all bond-2+ patterns < 3% of it; the 1024^4-class two-sided patterns are nil (one_sided == total to 4 digits) -> the feed is GEMM-class (2 GEMMs per read). Accumulated through the card's gate-only chain (first order, all sources l < L): rms grows to 5-5.5e-4 (55-60% of dk3 at depth) but OVERSHOOTS: beta 0.42-0.82 at L8-15, corr 0.25-0.37, energy 6-13% at beta -> the gate-only linear transport is NOT the card's real propagation of this content (it also enters the next planes/diagonal, which the card transports with the pair series and the star). DECISIVE NEXT: engine ablation NOFEED=1 (deploy frame, exact transport, everything else identical) vs the engine with the feed: if engine-without-feed ~ card (7-11%) the feed is THE card->engine gap.
- T-69b IN-CARD VALUE of the feed's k3 read alone (RETRO_K3_INJECT of the one-step read at layers 2-15, nothing else changed, deployed payload untouched; independent 4M scoring; inject_*_r{35,100}.log): r35 full card 2.7036e-8 -> x0.5 2.5396e-8 (-6.1%), x1 2.4268e-8 (-10.2%), x2 2.3547e-8 (-12.9%), x3 2.4880e-8 (-8.0%); host F2J-off 8.622e-8 -> 8.409 / 8.251 / 8.096 / 8.155e-8. r100: full card 2.7787e-8 -> x0.5 2.7350e-8 (-1.6%), x1 2.7310e-8 (-1.7%), x2 2.8358e-8 (+2.1%); host 7.696e-8 -> 7.746 / 7.836 / 8.134e-8 (WORSE). Reading: (1) the first derived correction on record that improves the FULL card with the deployed operator in place (the frozen tables broke it) - the feed is content the tape does not see; (2) read-only is incomplete: the optimal scale is net-dependent (x2 on r35, <= x1 on r100) and the r100 host worsens -> the feed's PLANE contribution (which drives the pair series -> post covariance -> the v channel that r100 needs) must be included; plane read built (plane_feed_plane_read.py, 9 GEMMs/pattern/layer, cert_plane_read_small.py PASSED 2e-7) with an in-card hook RETRO_K21_INJECT (+RETRO_K21_SCALE, RETRO_K21_INJECT_LAYERS). Engine ablation NOFEED (T-70): per-layer rel 0.063 (L4) -> 0.1166 (L15) without the feed vs 0.055 -> ~0.09 with it (reference run finishing) - the feed is the card->engine gap. MC certificate of the kernel coefficient running (cert_feed_kernel_mc.py).
- T-70 ENGINE ABLATION (r35, deploy frame, lmax 15, same code; engine_ablation_table_r35.txt, ENGINE_FEED_ABLATION_rank0035.json; per-unit k3 rel error vs 32M truth, k3u_l <-> mc_pre_k3[l] verified): card 3.5% (L2) / 7.3% (L8) / 10.8% (L12) / 11.1% (L14); engine WITH the feed 3.1 / 4.4 / 6.4 / 7.3%; engine WITHOUT the feed 3.3 / 4.9 / 7.6 / 8.0%. So the feed is a correctly-directed correction as large as the engine's whole residual (|ref - nofeed| = 3.7-6.3% of k3 at L8-14; corr +0.45..+0.56 with the no-feed residual; 14-29% of its energy) but it is only ~20% of the card->engine gap in k3-error points (0.7 of 3.8 at L14): the engine without the feed still beats the card by ~3 points. The other ~80% of the gap is elsewhere in the engine: candidates (i) the star's distinct entries pushed for the full depth (the residual carry closed 15-25% of the gap), (ii) the engine's SELF-SUPPLIED fourth-order planes (exact pushes; the card's are 13-63% low) feeding the K21 emission's Edgeworth weights, (iii) born distinct content beyond the (1,1) word (0.3% of k3, ruled out). Test for (ii): engine with the card's fourth-order planes each layer. PLANE READ (plane_feed_plane_read.py; PLANE_READ_rank00{35,100}.json): the feed's next-layer plane contribution vs the 8M truth plane residual (off-diagonal, 1M entries): beta 0.8-1.4, corr 0.17-0.37, energy explained 3-12% (r35) / 6-12% (r100), rising with depth; diagonal == k3 read to 0.5-1%.
- T-69c IN-CARD VALUE of the FULL one-step feed (plane read incl. its diagonal, RETRO_K21_INJECT at layers 2-15, deployed payload untouched, independent 4M; inject_plane_*_r{35,100}.log): r35 full card 2.7036e-8 -> x1 2.3685e-8 (-12.4%), x2 2.5397e-8 (-6.1%) - the optimum is now at the physical scale x1 (the k3-only form peaked at x2); host 8.622e-8 -> 8.305e-8 (-3.7%). r100 full card 2.7787e-8 -> x1 2.7027e-8 (-2.7%), x0.5 2.6920e-8 (-3.1%); host 7.696e-8 -> 7.864e-8 (+2.2%, worse) / 7.732e-8 (+0.5%). Bill of this form in-card: ~9 GEMMs per layer for the (0,1,0) pattern's plane read (+ ~1 for the (0,0,1) pattern) = ~+12% util for 14 layers -> at -12.4%/-2.7% raw the adjusted score does NOT improve; the k3-only form (3 GEMMs/layer, +4% util) at -10.2%/-1.7% is marginal. The value must rise (r100-class nets) or the bill fall before this is a candidate. The full card gains 3x more than the host on r35 and gains while the host loses on r100: the feed is content perpendicular to the operator's tape, which the operator complements.

- T-72 THE FOURTH-ORDER PLANES ARE THE LEVER (r35, independent 4M, RETRO_FORCE_PLANES: pre_k22/pre_k31 := 8M truth K22_l/K31_l at the consumption point, everything else the card's own; inject_planes_truth_r35.log, inject_k22_truth_r35.log, inject_planes_mix*_r35.log): host F2J-off 8.622e-8 -> 2.084e-8 (-76%; BELOW the deployed full card 2.704e-8) with truth K22+K31; K22 alone 2.730e-8 (-68%); F2J-on collapses (6.6e-8 / 7.2e-8: the payload over-corrects any corrected host, as always -> refit or off). So: deep k3 (and the host) is governed by the fourth-order PLANE supply; the card's planes are 2-3x too small at depth; the engine's cheap self-pushed chain (fourth_push_1024 + fourth_emission_1024, 1024^3-class) has 17-33% plane error. NEXT: engine planes forced in-card (deployable class) -> if the host falls to ~3-4e-8, implement the engine's plane chain in the card (few GEMMs/layer), then k4/k3 tables + feed on top, F2J refit -> panel -> virgin gate -> fire.

Engine NOBIGPAIR ablation running (is the pair term needed at all?).
- T-73 THE LEARNED OPERATOR IS THE FOURTH-ORDER PLANE DEFECT (perpendicular judge, PERP_JUDGE_G29.json; final-layer vectors, independent 4M): the engine-planes host effect has corr +0.91 with the deployed operator's correction and 84% of its energy inside the operator's span; its perpendicular remainder correlates +0.06 (r35) / -0.03 (r100) with truth - deployed (ceiling 0.4% / 0.1%). Truth planes: 87-88% inside, perp +0.35 / +0.15 (12% / 2.4%). The feed: 0% inside (perpendicular), perp +0.27 / +0.13 (7% / 1.7%) = its measured in-card value. And corr(truth - deployed, truth - truth-plane host) = +0.90: the card's leftover after the operator IS the leftover after perfect planes. So a plane chain in-card + operator refit would land near the deployed card (the redundancy law again); the plane chain is the DERIVED replacement of the learned operator (no learning) at ~engine-class accuracy (host 3.3e-8 r35 / 3.8e-8 r100 vs deployed 2.7e-8), not a board gain by itself. DECOMPOSITION AFTER TRUTH PLANES (r35, F2J off): + truth k3 -> 1.085e-8; + truth v -> 1.268e-8; + both -> 2.2e-9 (floor); truth v+k3 WITHOUT planes -> 2.685e-8. Own effects on the truth-plane host judged against truth - deployed: k3 +0.43 (ceiling 18.7%), v +0.35 (12.4%), v+k3 +0.56 (31%) - all perpendicular to the operator. => THE GOAL DECOMPOSES AS planes (derived, in-span) -> k3 given planes (engine's accumulated 6% at L12 with truth planes; perpendicular) -> v given planes+k3. NOBIGPAIR: the engine's only 1024^4 term contributes nothing (k3 error identical to 4 digits at L8/L14) -> the plane chain is ~16 GEMMs/layer. Engine chain 3 running (truth fourth-order + SECOND_ORDER Edgeworth / TENSOR_K4 insertions): which truncation is the engine's accumulated k3 error.

Truth post planes generated for r35 L8/L12 (mc_post_planes_mps.py, 4M, 51 s; self-check vs the 8M pre planes: K21/K22 corr 0.998-0.9999, K31 0.98-0.99 = K31's own sampling noise is ~10-15% at these sizes; k3 rel 1.3-1.9%).
- T-76 THE PLANE CHAIN'S FAULT, SPLIT (fourth_split_cert.py, FOURTH_SPLIT_rank0035.json; r35 L8 and L12; truth 32M univariates + 8M pre planes in; truth 4M POST planes as the emission target; 8M pre planes at l+1 as the push target): EMISSION (pre -> post): K21 exact (rel 1.5%), k4 exact (1%), K22 good (rel 7-9%, corr 0.998), K31 BROKEN: ratio 7.8 / 11.0, corr 0.33 / 0.36 (8-11x too large, wrong direction) - the (3,1) connected cumulant is a small remainder of mu31 - 3 v cov (cancellation amplifies the emission's moment error). PUSH (truth post -> pre l+1): K22 ratio 0.97 / 0.94, corr 0.97 / 0.95, rel 25 / 32%; K31 ratio 0.33 / 0.26, corr 0.35 / 0.30 (rel 94-96%); k4 rel 28 / 37% - the plane-supported push drops the post tensor's (2,1,1) and (1,1,1,1) classes = the same "lost born distinct fourth-order content" as the card's k4 defect (T-49). So: K22 = emission fine + push loses a quarter; K31 = both stages broken; the engine's accumulated planes (K22 corr 0.95-0.98, K31 corr 0.5) are these two faults compounded. Deployable plane accuracy needs (i) a K31 emission that survives the cancellation (second-order Edgeworth / direct cumulant map), (ii) the distinct fourth-order content in the push (the born-word plane feed, T-49: block patterns carry 33% / 49% of the path contribution at 12 GEMM-eq/layer).
- T-76b IN-CARD PLANE-ACCURACY LADDER (r35, F2J off, independent 4M): card 8.62e-8; engine K22 only (card K31) 3.10e-8; engine K22 + engine K31 3.32e-8 (the engine's K31 HURTS); truth K22 + engine K31 2.86e-8; engine K22 + truth K31 2.47e-8; truth both 2.08e-8. K31 and K22 accuracy are worth comparable amounts (~0.6-0.8e-8 each). Emission-side diagnosis: fourth_emission_1024 builds mu31 with r3v = raw3 = the GAUSSIAN E[phi^3] (no Edgeworth corrections) while M31, r1, r2 carry first-order corrections -> mu31 is wrong at first order in kappa3, and the connected K31 = mu31 - 3 v cov is a small remainder -> 8-11x garbage. Fix under test: r3 = c3[0] + (k3/6)/sd^3 c3[3] + (k4/24)/sd^4 c3[4] + (k3^2/72)/sd^6 c3[6] (Stein: c3[q] = sd^q E[d^q phi^3]).
- T-77 K31 EMISSION FIXED (run_tensor_recursion_v4.py FOURTH_R3FIX=1; fourth_split_cert.py r35): with E[phi^3] carrying the same Edgeworth corrections as the other moments (r3 = c3[0] + (k3/6)/sd^3 c3[3] + (k4/24)/sd^4 c3[4] + (k3^2/72)/sd^6 c3[6]), the one-step emission of the (3,1) post plane from truth inputs goes from ratio 7.8 / 11.0, corr 0.33 / 0.36 to ratio 1.000 / 1.001, corr 0.998 / 0.995, rel err 6.4 / 10.1% (L8 / L12); K22 (7-9%) and k4 (1%) unchanged. The connected (3,1) plane is 8-9% of its disconnected part 3 v cov (a 1% moment error is 0.1x K31), so the uncorrected Gaussian third moment (a first-order-in-kappa3 error) was the whole garbage. One derivation-level bug in the fourth-order emission - the engine's K31 was never right, and its garbage K31 also fed the K22/M22 emission. Remaining plane fault = the PUSH (drops the post tensor's (2,1,1)/(1,1,1,1) classes: K22 25-32%, K31 90%). Running the engine with the fix (accumulated planes) -> force in-card.
- T-77b STRUCTURE OF THE PUSH RESIDUAL (push_residual_rank0035_L{8,12}.npz = truth pre-plane at l+1 minus the plane-supported push of the truth post planes at l): K22 missing 25 / 32% of truth, K31 missing 94 / 96%, k4 missing 28 / 37% (L8->9 / L12->13); the missing part is UNCORRELATED with the pushed planes (corr -0.001..+0.036; truth-on-pushed slope 1.00 for K22) - not a rescale; moderately structured: rank-8 holds 52-65% of its energy, rank-32 63-83%, rank-128 80-94%. It is the post tensor's distinct (2,1,1)/(1,1,1,1) content contracted into the next planes - the object T-49 priced as the born-word plane feed (block patterns 33% / 49% of the path contribution, 12 GEMM-eq/layer). The k4 miss (28-37%) is the univariate table's (T-48) source.
- T-77c PRIOR ART CHECK: the K31 emission fix is the same as teammate A's T-49 "g24fix" (G23 LANE §18: mu31 with the Edgeworth-corrected E[x^3]; certified vs the card's own K31 target 0.24 -> 0.999 at L5). A found that in the ACCUMULATED engine the fix makes the carried K31 WORSE (0.59 -> 0.34 at L8) and K22/k4 slightly worse, because neither engine nor card carries the born fourth-order path content in the PUSH and the uncorrected raw3 happened to compensate part of it. T-76/T-77 now separate the two cleanly with one-step truth-post-plane certificates: emission right (K31 corr 0.998), push wrong (the distinct classes). The push's born content: block patterns (GEMM-class) carry 33% / 49% of the path words' plane contribution (T-49); the interleaved patterns are 1024^4 - UNLESS the post correlation is low-rank (the missing residual is 52-65% rank-8; the truth K22 plane is 96-98% rank-8): with rho_post ~ rank-K every path word becomes a K^3-term GEMM chain. Checking the post rho spectrum.
- T-77d THE PUSH'S MISSING CONTENT IS NOT LOW-RANK-DERIVABLE (r35, 8M cov2): post/pre correlation matrices (off-diagonal, live units) hold only 42-58% of their energy in rank 8, 77-87% in rank 32, 90-95% in rank 128 (rms |rho| 0.05-0.11, top eigenvalue 19-61). So the interleaved 4-path patterns (abab, abba: 1024^4) do not collapse to GEMM chains (rank-K would need K^3 terms per word with K ~ 32), and Hutchinson sketches of the bilinear forms need ~10^3 probes. The born fourth-order distinct content in the push is the same wall the card's CP4 (2048 symmetric terms for a ~10^6-term pair-centered object) loses half of - consistent with [finwidth-deepmoment-crack-walltime-walled] and [cap-ladder-loses-once-billed]. DEPLOYABLE plane accuracy therefore tops out near "engine K22 (emission + block-pattern push) + the card's K31": host ~3.1e-8 (r35) F2J-off; truth-class planes (2.08e-8) are not reachable at GEMM cost. BOARD ARITHMETIC (honest): engine-K22 host 3.10e-8 / ~3.5e-8 (r100 est.) at +~10% util; an operator refit on it, by the two-table precedent (removes ~30% of what is left), lands ~2.2-2.5e-8 raw -> adj ~-5% vs the bank - below the meaningful-fire bar and unproven (the perpendicular judge says ~0); the derived no-operator card at ~3.1e-8 / util 0.7 is adj-worse. The bank (1.5e-8) is out of reach of every measured piece; the goal (1e-8) needs truth-class planes + k3, i.e. the 1024^4 born content.
- T-77e ENGINE WITH THE EMISSION FIX, ACCUMULATED (r35, FOURTH_R3FIX=1 deploy frame; engine_planes_rank0035_r3fix.npz; inject_planes_r3fix / r3fixK22_only): carried planes vs truth K22 ratio 0.92 / 0.86 corr 0.97 / 0.95, K31 0.34 / 0.24 corr 0.37 / 0.27 at L8 / L12 (unfixed engine: K22 0.96 / 0.92, K31 0.59 / 0.56) -> in-card host 3.48e-8 (K22+K31) / 3.35e-8 (K22 only) vs the unfixed engine's 3.32e-8 / 3.10e-8. Replicates teammate A's T-49 observation exactly: the correct emission removes the accidental compensation of the push's missing born content, so end to end the chain gets worse. The plane ladder's deployable ceiling stays at engine-K22 (unfixed) + card K31 = 3.10e-8 on r35. Block closed; decision for Matt in HANDOFF_CURRENT.md.

- T-80e V-ONLY SUPPLIES (r35 unless noted, operator ON, independent 4M): the truth-plane card's v minus the card's v (|dv|/|true dv| 0.60-0.83, corr +0.50..+0.77) injected as a v table: beta 1 -> 7.30e-8 (host 1.84e-7!), beta 0.5 -> 4.04e-8; the engine-K22 card's v (corr +0.40..+0.73): 8.13e-8 / 4.45e-8 - both far WORSE than the card, although each has a smaller v error than the card. The half-truth-v ceiling (-25% / -35%) stands as the target of N0.1e; its source is the unit-specific k3/k4 error through the univariate map = the wall. Door closed for tonight.

- T-81c SKEW-PLANE ACCURACY LADDER on the truth-plane host (r35; K21 off-diagonal rel err / k3 rel err vs truth at L6/8/10/12/14): card 0.086/0.061, 0.094/0.073, 0.110/0.091, 0.125/0.108, 0.133/0.111; truth fourth-order planes (tp) 0.076/0.044 ... 0.105/0.065; tp + cum4 feed 0.083/0.056 ... 0.107/0.067 (the cum4 feed slightly WORSENS the K21 plane while cutting the host -8%); tp + plane-to-distinct feed 0.072/0.041 ... 0.094/0.059 (host 1.803e-8); tp + both 0.079/0.053 ... 0.096/0.061 (host 1.697e-8); tp + residual carry (window 1-6, default arena) 0.059/0.032 at L6 but 0.167/0.160 at L14 (host 4.30e-8: the arena schedule drops at depth). Nothing on record takes the deep K21 plane below ~9%; the 2e-8 route via the pair consumer needs ~3%. Consumer dependence on fourth-order planes: truth K21 with the CARD's K22/K31 inside the consumer -> beta 0.5 -20.6% becomes -5.8% (2.548e-8); the 'true' file (truth states) at beta 0.5: 2.339e-8. ANSWER TO MATT (lowest deployable MSE): the deployed card (2.70 / 2.78e-8 offline; 2.94e-8 board); f32 pack identical raw at -4.9% util; the feed -9.2% on r35 / +1.4% on r100 (32M frame).
- T-82 ENGINE-CLASS SHIFTS FAIL r100 (operator ON, beta 0.5, independent 4M; deployed 2.779e-8): engine k3u shift 3.261e-8 (+17%), engine dv-hat + k3u 2.917e-8 (+5%); the alignment of the engine's k3 shift on r100 decays with depth (+0.82 / +0.69 / +0.56 at L8 / L12 / L14). => Matt's 2e-8 bar needs supplies aligned >~ 0.9 on EVERY net; the full-tensor engine (0.8 on r35, 0.6-0.8 on r100) is not enough even if it were deployable. The remaining route with real odds: the born fourth-order/third-order plane content via GEMM-class block-pattern feeds (T-49: direction corr 0.95-0.97 with the full path contribution at n=32, magnitude 33-49%) with learned per-pair multipliers fit on truth planes across many nets -> planes at few-% accuracy -> the pair consumer's half-scale variance correction. Premise test next: alignment of the block-pattern feed with the actual push residual at 1024 (push_residual_rank0035_L{8,12}.npz).
- T-83 THE BORN-WORD PLANE FEED IS DEAD AT 1024 (block_feed_1024.py; GEMM-class block-pattern feed of the 4-path born words, CERTIFIED against the dense sum over all adjacent-distinct 4-tuples at n=24: rel err 2e-14 for K22 and K31; premise test with TRUTH pre m/v/C at L8 and L12 against the actual push residual = truth pre-plane at l+1 minus the plane-supported push of the truth post planes): feed / missing ratio 0.010-0.015, corr +0.07..+0.09, best scale 5-9, missing left after the scaled feed 0.996-0.998 - the same at bond order 4. The Gaussian-born 4-path words (which dominate a Gaussian ReLU quadruple's kappa4 in the n=32 census at rho 0.3-0.5) are ~1% of the missing fourth-order plane content at 1024 (rho ~0.05-0.1: path words scale as rho^3). The push residual is therefore NON-Gaussian-born / inherited fourth-order distinct content (third-order pre-content squared, inherited kappa4 distinct entries) = what a fourth-order TENSOR carrier (the card's CP4) represents. The learned-multiplier-on-block-patterns route (proposed to Matt) is closed by this certificate; the memo's Rank 5 feed likewise.
- T-84 CP4 RANK DOES NOT HELP (r35, CP4_CAP 2048 / 4096 / 8192; k4dump_cp4cap*): fourth-order pre-plane ratio vs truth at L8 K22 0.58 / 0.56 / 0.56, K31 0.44 / 0.43 / 0.43, k4 0.58 / 0.56 / 0.56 (corr unchanged 0.98 / 0.70); host 8.62 / 9.05 / 9.22e-8 (worse) at util 0.600 / 0.666 / 0.762. The card's fourth-order shortfall (planes and k4 at 0.4-0.6 of truth with the RIGHT direction) is not carrier capacity: it is how the card ASSEMBLES/EMITS its fourth-order content each layer (same conclusion as T-45 from the other side). Hypothesis (T-85 test): `_reset_stars` fits pairing_alpha = <K22 target, v v^T + 2 C∘C>/<..> by least squares and subtracts 3 alpha v v from k4 and alpha-scaled planes - a projection that also removes the part of the CONNECTED content aligned with the disconnected plane (the truth K22 is collective/rank-8), producing exactly a direction-preserving under-scale of K22 and k4 with the same layer profile.
- T-85 THE CARD'S FOURTH-ORDER SHORTFALL IS ALONG THE PAIRING PLANE (r35): corr(truth K22 - card K22, v v^T + 2 C∘C) = +0.89 / +0.91 / +0.90 at L6 / L10 / L14 (79-82% of the missing energy along it); the card's K22 is 0.999-correlated with that plane, the truth's 0.96-0.985; the k4 shortfall is +0.91 correlated with v^2. => the card's `_reset_stars` least-squares pairing_alpha projection removes the connected content's component along the disconnected plane (the same single defect behind the k4 table 0.99 -> 0.40 and the K22 0.92 -> 0.41). Testing an alpha override (1.0 / half / 0). Partial-scale kurtosis with the operator ON: truth k4 at mix 0.5 r35 3.37e-8 (+25%), r100 4.38e-8 (+57%) - the fourth-order channel is fully owned by the operator, so an in-card fix pays on the board only through better third-order alignment (k3/K21 -> the variance consumer), not directly. (The k4-table "mix" arm was invalid: the rescale path ignores RETRO_FORCE_MIX.)
- T-85b THE CARD'S FOURTH-ORDER PLANES ARE A ONE-PARAMETER OBJECT (r35, RETRO_PAIRING_ALPHA override in `_reset_stars`; k4dump_alpha*): alpha := 1.0 -> explosion (K22 ratio 830-4047; alpha's natural scale is ~1e-3); alpha x0.5 -> K22 / K31 / k4 ratios 0.15 / 0.12 / 0.14 at L8 (from 0.58 / 0.44 / 0.58), host 2.5e-7; alpha := 0 -> 0.05 / 0.07 / 0.05, host 3.0e-7. So the card's fourth-order planes are essentially alpha x (v v^T + 2 C∘C) (corr 0.999 with the pairing plane): a per-layer least-squares scalar fitted on the pair series' own k22 target, which converges self-consistently to 40-60% of the truth (the k4 table = the alpha correction; the "closure-coadaptation basin"). The engine's plane chain (no such projection) reaches K22 0.92-0.98 -> the deployable fix is the plane-chain port (in-span for the board). K21 ERROR STRUCTURE (truth - card, off-diagonal, corr with candidate planes at L6 / L10 / L14): v m^T +0.34 / +0.45 / +0.48, v k3^T +0.35 / +0.45 / +0.47 (row-variance weighting), the card's own K21 +0.23-0.30, C / C∘C / K22 / K31 / m v^T ~ 0: no single simple plane explains it - the skew-plane error is distributed (consistent with its rank structure). HONEST STATE for the 2e-8 bar: the consumer route needs K21 at ~3% (card 8-13%, best derived ~9%); the (dv, dk3) route needs alignment >~ 0.9 on every net (engine 0.6-0.8; tape 0.45). No candidate.
- T-86 REMOVING THE ENGINE'S COHERENT UNDER-SCALE LIFTS ALIGNMENT TO THE BAR (engine ref k3u vs 32M truth; frozen per-layer table = truth-on-engine slope fitted on r35: 1.009 (L4) .. 1.030 (L8) .. 1.047 (L12) .. 1.060 (L14); applied HELD-OUT to r100): r100 skew-shift alignment with the true dk3 raw +0.84 / +0.84 / +0.82 / +0.81 / +0.69 / +0.56 at L4/6/8/10/12/14 -> with the frozen table +0.85 / +0.88 / +0.90 / +0.91 / +0.88 / +0.84 (own-slope +0.86-0.91); r35 in-sample +0.80 / +0.80 / +0.76 -> +0.90 / +0.91 / +0.92 at L8/12/14. Stepping back to the corpus reviewers' reports before choosing the next thread.

- T-87h KAPPA3-SQUARED CLASS CEILING (K31 fine structure regressed on 19 GEMM-class products of the truth third-order planes and states; fit on r35, applied held-out to r100): corr in-sample +0.43 / +0.55 / +0.65 at L6/10/14, held-out +0.39 / +0.56 / +0.57. - T-87j FIXED DELTAS VS SELF-CONSISTENT MIX: injecting HALF of the (engine-table K21 minus base-card K21) as a fixed per-layer delta at layers 2-14 explodes the card (r35 7.27e-8, r100 5.3e-7; ek-card delta 6.07e-8 / 9.2e-8), although the alignment of the base-relative delta is 0.8. Cause: a delta computed against the BASE trajectory double-counts once earlier layers are already corrected (the corrected card's plane is closer to the supply than the base's), and the plane channel accumulates it. The truth 'mix' forcing that works (forced = current + 0.5 (supply - current)) is self-consistent; a deployable supply computed alongside the card is naturally of that form. With the operator ON: 2.90e-8 / 4.60e-8 (engine), 3.07e-8 / 4.58e-8 (ek) - worse than the deployed card. PLANES ARE IN-SPAN (the operator double-counts them), while the per-unit STATES v and k3 at half scale are perpendicular (T-80j). - T-90 COST CENSUS AND THE CAP DIAL ([instruction removed]). The deployed card's 0.600 (CENSUS_RAW.json, r35): CP3 Winograd arena push 0.27 (grows with depth: 1.6e10 at L3 -> 7.4e10 at L14), CP4 read + push 0.106, quartic star push 0.082, covariance push f64 0.059, cubic star push 0.055, pair-series einsum 0.018, operator ~0. Cutting the CP3 cap is NOT a dial in phase 2: cap 4096 explodes the raw 10x (old card 3.36e-7 F2J off, chain host 3.32e-7) at util 0.456; caps 2048/1024/512 0.41/0.39/0.39 and 9e-7..2e-6; a matching regenerated arena (RETRO_ARENA_PUSH/READ) crashes on read-shape mismatches at caps 8192/4096. A ~0.20-util card = covariance (f32) + in-card K22 chain (~0.12) + a PLANE-ONLY third order (no CP3/CP4/quartic star) = the competitor's compute class; its raw is what run_plane_recursion.py graded. Looking that up.
- T-90b PLANE-ONLY THIRD ORDER IS DEAD (tracker line 18 / 332: the standalone plane recursion is 14x worse; the pushed plane is a cancellation between plane-slice terms and distinct entries, removing the distinct content breaks it). So the CP3 carrier is load-bearing and its cost (0.27 at cap 16384) sets our architecture's floor: with the chain replacing CP4 + the quartic star (-0.19 + 0.10) and f32 covariance (-0.03), the card is ~0.50 at cap 16384 (raw ~3.1e-8 F2J off -> adjusted ~1.5e-8, -13% vs the bank; with the refit operator ~1.35e-8, -24%). A cap-4096 CP3 with a CORRECT drop schedule (not the sealed arena's) would give ~0.30; testing whether the cap explosion (T-90) is the arena schedule (relaxed arena, read = push - 2048).
- T-90c CAP LADDER BLOCKED BY THE ARENA: regenerated arenas at 8192/7680/4096/3840 (read = push-1024, -2048, or the sealed 15:14 block ratio) all crash on block-shape mismatches in _cp3_winograd_arena_depth4; only the sealed 15360/14336 and the T-51 30720/28672 layouts run. Decision (14:50): package the ~0.50-util chain card (in-card K22 chain replacing CP4 + the quartic star; f32 covariance; refit operator or F2J off), expected adjusted 1.35-1.55e-8 (-13..-24% vs the bank); the competitor's 4e-9 class is out of reach of this architecture's third order.
- T-91 THE CAP EXPLOSION IS PHYSICS, NOT THE ARENA (RETRO_PLAIN_CP3=1: plain GEMM push/read in place of the sealed Winograd arena; r35, independent 4M): cap 16384 plain reproduces the deployed card exactly (F2J on 2.7038e-8, off 8.622e-8) at util 0.771 (the arena saves 0.17); lean card (CP4 cap 256, f32 covariance) at cap 4096 -> host 3.30e-7 (util 0.447), 2048 -> 8.90e-7 (0.379), 1024 -> 1.64e-6 (0.335); with the chain 9.3e-7 / 1.65e-6. Dropping carrier terms breaks the third-order cancellation structure (T-51's partial-carry law); the carrier's full rank is load-bearing, so the CP3 push (0.27 arena / 0.47 plain) is this architecture's cost floor. A competitor-class 0.2-util card needs a different third-order representation.
- T-91b BILLING: flopscope bills f16 matmuls exactly as f32 (1.0 x 2N^3; f64 2.0x) - no precision lever below f32. SYNTHESIS (15:20, for Matt): under adjusted = raw x util, the phase-2 blocks compose to ~1.45e-8 (chain card: in-card K22 chain replacing CP4 + quartic star, f32 covariance, ~0.50 util, raw ~2.9-3.1e-8) = -20% vs the bank; 4e-9 at 0.50 needs raw 8e-9 (the half-truth per-unit state class), and a 0.2-util card needs a third-order representation 4x cheaper than the CP3 carrier, which the corpus does not contain. Packaging the chain card next: fnp port of k22_chain into the estimator_cap.py lineage.
- T-92 RETRACTION OF THE 1.45e-8 ESTIMATE (15:40): the chain without its K31 channel costs +8% raw (r35 3.35e-8, r100 3.87e-8 F2J off); the chain supplying ALL fourth-order objects (CP4 + quartic star removable) costs +35-40% raw (T-91 runs chain_all: 4.18e-8 / 5.20e-8). Priced honestly: (a) chain K22 + CP4/quartic kept + f32: util ~0.71, refit F2J-on 2.88/2.90e-8 -> adjusted ~2.05e-8; (b) chain-all + f32: util ~0.52, raw 4.2-5.2e-8 -> ~2.2e-8; both WORSE than the bank (1.78e-8). The chain's host gain is the same fourth-order fix the deployed operator already performs on the old host (in-span, T-73). Only the f32 pack banks (1.70e-8, -4.9%, below the bar). No chain-card package. The 4e-9 target has no measured block in the corpus; open threads: SOL (3,1)-plane ask, transported state corrector gate (feat dumps on 8 nets exist: feat_derived_retro-*.npz).
- T-93 THE COMPETITOR'S CLASS IS SPHERICAL CUBATURE (Matt 16:30; whest_phase1_writeup_skibidi_toilet.pdf, submission #325170, phase 1): bias-free ReLU nets are positively homogeneous, so E_x[M(x)] = E[chi_n] x E_{u in S^{n-1}}[M(u)] exactly; the sphere integral is evaluated on a tight spherical 4-design (all 129 real Kerdock MUBs of R^256, antipodal closure, 66,048 points; layer 1 by FWHT, the antipodal half of layer 2 free), exact layer-1 mean and layer-2 variance snaps (arc-cosine kernel; a 0.195% coherent snap error raised final MSE 4.2x), pilot-driven neuron pruning (cut ~80% of columns, dropped neurons compensated), Strassen-Winograd L4/L5 (0.74x), an exact sparse cold path; score = V c / B is FLAT in the point count; phase-1 per-point variance V = 0.0185 (MC 0.042). Phase 2 (#329661): raw 1.854e-8 at util 0.218 -> 4.04e-9. At 1024 x 16: 513 real MUBs exist (n = 4^5) but the full 5-design (1.05M points) is ~9x the budget, so a sub-design (not tight) or unions of antipodal orthonormal bases (3-designs) are the affordable forms. Prototype: public_g30_cubature_v1/cubature_proto.py (ortho bases, radial factor, both snaps; grades V per point and adj = V c / B).
- T-93b CUBATURE PROTOTYPE AT 1024x16 (public_g30_cubature_v1/cubature_proto.py; r35, 32M truth, noise-corrected; per-point variance V = MSE x k; adjusted = V c / B with c = 15 dense layers, 0.59x for Winograd + pruning): plain Gaussian MC k=8192: final 9.91e-6, V 0.081 (matches the board's MC line 1.13e-6 at the full budget); antipodal orthonormal bases + radial factor, no snaps: V 0.049; + exact layer-1 mean and layer-2 arc-cosine variance snaps: V 0.045 (b=4) / 0.037 (b=8, k=16384: final 2.24e-6). Best case adjusted ~3.1e-7 - 20x WORSE than our bank and 80x above the competitor's 4.04e-9. The exact layer-1 formula matches the truth to the noise floor (col norms / sqrt(2 pi): 2.28e-8 vs se^2 2.13e-8), so the orientation and the frame are right. At this shape the degree >= 4 harmonics hold ~half the variance; the paper's own phase-1 gain was 2.3x over MC and its phase-2 score is 280x over MC, so the phase-2 entry is a different (or heavily augmented) method. The closure + operator class (ours: 38x over MC raw, 64x adjusted) remains the strongest measured class on this machine.
- T-94 TRANSPORTED STATE-CORRECTOR GATE: DEAD (transported_dv_gate.py; derived host = chain + carry on the 8 panel nets, feat_derived_retro-*.npz; ridge, leave-one-net-out, held-out corr mean/min): dv local 0.17/0.14 (L10) .. 0.19/0.04 (L14); + the ORACLE previous-layer dv transported through W^2 (three gate weightings) 0.17-0.20 - the true previous-layer variance error does not predict the next layer's; the autonomous rollout equals local. dk3 local 0.35-0.42 at L10-14; dk4 local 0.55-0.64. Nothing near the 0.65-0.9 bars: the per-unit variance error is born fresh at each layer (T-80c's four-neuron content), not transported. 8-NET DERIVED-HOST PANEL (chain + carry, no feed, F2J off, util 0.82-0.84): 2.39 / 3.16 / 3.14 / 3.39 / 2.36 / 2.31 / 4.14 / 3.28e-8 (35/100/150/200/250/300/400/500), mean 3.02e-8: not a bank. STATE (17:00): no measured route to 4e-9 on this machine; the competitor's phase-2 method is unknown (his phase-1 cubature class measures 2.2x over MC at this shape, T-93b).
- T-95 THE COMPRESSION ESCAPE HAS A SPECTRUM (r35, card gates, cohort factor X_b(l) = M_{b->l} (g_b * C_b), map M_{b->l} = prod of W^T G): rank for 90/99/99.9% of the energy - X: age 1 138/302/461, age 2 115/250/381, age 4 86/186/284, age 6 70/150/228, age 8 58/125/191, age 10 50/108/166 (cohort L2; L4/L6 cohorts similar or lower); M: age 2 301/525/676, age 4 161/313/437, age 6 111/223/322, age 8 83/172/253, age 10 68/142/212. Products of gated random matrices decay steeply, so old cohorts of the CP3 bank live in low-rank subspaces. Per-cohort low-rank form (P_b = U_p R_p, Q_b = U_q R_q; push only the U's; read ((U_p R_p)o(U_p R_p)) Lambda R_q^T U_q^T): ~(2 r_x + 3 r_y)/1024 GEMM-eq per cohort-layer vs 4 -> the carrier 0.27 -> ~0.08-0.10 at 99% energy. Nothing is dropped (T-51's partial-removal catastrophe was dropping coherent terms), only truncated in singular directions. If the K21 planes hold at <= 5% error: card ~0.40 util at raw 2.94e-8 -> adjusted ~1.2e-8 (-34% vs the bank), and with CP4 compressed the same way ~0.33 -> ~9.7e-9. Decisive test: offline rank-truncation of the dumped bank cohorts vs the exact read.
- T-95b UNIFORM-RANK TRUNCATION OF THE BANK COHORTS (bankcp_r35 dumps L4/8/12/14; cohort_lowrank_study.py; exact read reproduced to 7e-6): the P factors keep 99.9%+ of their energy at rank 256 from age 2, but the Q factors of the YOUNGEST cohorts do not (age-1 Q = W^T G, full rank: 62% at rank 256), and the K21 read error at uniform rank is set by them: rank 256 -> 15% (L14) / 36% (L4); rank 512 -> 6.4% / 15%. Age-dependent schedule needed (young cohorts near full rank, old ones 192-256); the old cohorts dominate the bank's cost, so the saving survives.
- T-95c AGE-SCHEDULED COHORT TRUNCATION (bank dumps r35; P/Q ranks by cohort age): schedule A (P 512/256/256/192.., Q 1024/768/512/384/256..): K21 read err 7.6% (L4) / 2.7% (L8) / 1.8% (L12) / 1.6% (L14), k3 5.8/1.9/1.3/1.1%, cost 10/16, 15/32, 19/48, 22/56 GEMM-eq; schedule B (P 512/384/256/256/192.., Q 1024/1024/768/512/384/256): 4.6 / 1.8 / 1.0 / 0.9% (k3 3.5/1.2/0.7/0.7%), cost 12.5/16, 17/32, 22/48, 24/56; schedule C (tighter): 2.7 / 1.1 / 0.5 / 0.3% at 15/16, 21/32, 27/48, 30/56. The deep layers dominate the cost, so the carrier ~0.27 -> ~0.12-0.14 (B) with read errors 1-2% at depth (the card's own K21 error is 8-13%). Card estimate: 0.60 -> ~0.45 (with f32 covariance) at the deployed raw if the in-card recursion tolerates the truncation -> adjusted ~1.3e-8 (-26% vs the bank). Not the 4e-9 class by itself (stars 0.14 + covariance 0.03 + chain/CP4 >= 0.1 remain); the first real cost lever since the 0.6065 card. Next: implement the low-rank cohort bank in the retro instrument (RETRO_LOWRANK_CP3) and measure raw F2J on/off on r35/r100.
- T-96 LOW-RANK COHORT BANK, IN-CARD EMULATION (RETRO_LOWRANK_CP3 = "P ranks by age;Q ranks by age": after each push every cohort block is replaced by its rank-truncated SVD, so raw is exactly what a low-rank card gives; independent 4M; deployed r35 2.7038e-8 on / 8.622e-8 off, r100 2.7787e-8 / 7.696e-8): schedule B (512,384,256,256,192,192; 1024,1024,768,512,384,256): r35 3.224e-8 (+19%) / 8.98e-8 (+4.2%), r100 3.097e-8 (+11%) / 8.03e-8 (+4.4%); schedule C (768,512,384,256,256,192; 1024,1024,1024,768,512,384): r35 2.866e-8 (+6.0%) / 8.607e-8 (-0.2%), r100 2.870e-8 (+3.3%) / 7.538e-8 (-2.1%). The host tolerates C exactly; the +3-6% with the operator on is co-adaptation (refit recoverable). COST when the truncations are billed (Q = the transport map, full rank for 3 ages; randomized SVDs ~1 GEMM-eq per young cohort; old cohorts ~2.5 vs 4 GEMM-eq): carrier 0.27 -> ~0.20; card ~0.47 with f32 covariance -> adjusted ~1.35e-8 (-24% vs the bank). A real fire candidate, a day of fnp engineering (factored push/read, randomized SVD, cost certificate), not the 4e-9 class: even with CP4 compressed the same way the floor is ~0.4 (stars 0.14, covariance 0.03, chain/CP4 >= 0.05, misc).
- T-97 LOW-RANK COHORT BANK IN FLOPSCOPE (RETRO_LR_BANK; _lr_push/_lr_schedule/_lr_read/_lr_k3/_lr_grow; randomized range finder = billed matmuls + fnp.linalg.qr): CERTIFICATE full rank "1024;1024" reproduces the plain-push card exactly (r35 F2J on 2.70375e-8, off 8.6218e-8, util 0.7705). SCHEDULE C BILLED: util 0.8394 (> plain 0.7705, > arena 0.60) with raw 3.79e-8 / 9.28e-8 (the range finder without power iteration is less accurate than the exact SVD emulation of T-96). Why the estimate of T-96 was wrong: (i) a cohort's read is cheaper only when BOTH factors are low-rank (P factored with Q dense costs 4 + 4 r_p/1024 > 4), and Q = the transport map is full rank for 3 ages; (ii) each truncation is billed (0.6-2.5 GEMM-eq), ~13 GEMM-eq per cohort lifetime. Retracted. Remaining variant: P-only truncation at birth (one 0.6 GEMM-eq truncation, saving 2 - 4 r_p/1024 per cohort-layer), ceiling ~25% of the carrier (~0.07 util) if raw holds; only worth it on top of the Winograd arena path (-15% adjusted at best).
- T-97b P-ONLY COMPRESSION DEAD (RETRO_LR_BANK="256;1024": birth factor truncated to 256 once, map never): util 0.698 (from 0.7705 plain) but raw 1.25e-7 / 1.92e-7 (the randomized range finder at birth loses the plane). COMPRESSION CLOSED WITH RECEIPTS (T-95..T-97b): the cohort spectra decay, but a billed truncation plus the dense-map reads cost more than they save; the O(depth^2) push/read of the third-order cohorts is the price of the distinct third-order content in any factor form (probe / adjoint views are transposes of the same O(L^2) maps). The instrument keeps the LR path (certified at full rank) for future free-basis ideas.

Testing the no-drop residual carry on LATE windows with the operator kept.

- T-99 THE 4e-9 CLASS IS A LATE-LAYER PER-UNIT SUPPLY (r35, 32M truth states, independent 4M): chain host (F2J off, 3.09e-8): exact (v,k3,k4) at L13-15 only -> 1.204e-8 (-61%), at L9-15 -> 3.9e-9 (-87%). - T-100 THE COVARIANCE RESIDUAL IS LOW-RANK AND MEAN-ALIGNED (Keenan Pepper's phase-1 finding, main-1.pdf section 3.5 / wall 4, verified on our phase-2 card; E_l = C_truth(pre, 8M cov2) - C_card(pre); r35 / r100): |E|/|C| 0.6% (L4) -> 1.3-1.4% (L14); energy share of the top eigen-direction 0.04/0.03 (L4), 0.27/0.35 (L8), 0.52/0.49 (L12), 0.61/0.54 (L14); rank 2: 0.66/0.65 at L14; rank 5: 0.73/0.71; rank 16: 0.82/0.81; |<u1, mu-hat>| 0.83/0.81 at L14 (0.77 at L8), |<u2, mu-hat>| 0.45/0.52; eigenvalues paired +/- (lambda1 +0.26, lambda2 -0.07). The DIAGONAL of the rank-k residual vs the true per-unit dv (32M): L14 k=1 0.77/0.73, k=2 0.80/0.80, k=5 0.85/0.84, k=16 0.90/0.89 (L12: 0.72-0.86); mu-hat^2 alone 0.44. So the per-unit variance error (T-80's universal lever, T-94's 'born fresh, unpredictable') is the diagonal of a 2-16 direction covariance residual whose top direction is largely the mean vector. Keenan: corrections convert only where the residual is low-rank; his converting mechanism was a learned rank-16 per-neuron-factor write into the covariance STATE (co-adapted compensator). Next: the oracle value of a rank-k covariance write at half scale with the operator kept (RETRO_FORCE=Ck).
- T-100b THE LOW-RANK COVARIANCE WRITE HURTS (RETRO_FORCE=Ck: true residual's top-k eigen-directions added to the pre-covariance at mix s, self-consistent; operator ON / OFF; deployed r35 2.704e-8 / 8.62e-8, r100 2.779e-8 / 7.70e-8): r35 k=2 s=0.5 3.88e-8 / 1.14e-7; k=2 s=1.0 5.09e-8 / 1.34e-7; k=16 s=0.5 3.14e-8 / 8.34e-8; k=2 s=0.5 layers 9-15 3.34e-8; r100 k=2 s=0.5 2.64e-8 (-5%), k=16 s=0.5 2.77e-8 (0%). The OFF-DIAGONAL part of the true low-rank residual is poison for the card's co-adapted covariance/plane state (Keenan's wall 5: the converting write is a compensator, not the truth), while the diagonal-only half-truth v gives -25/-35% (T-80). Testing the diagonal of the rank-k residual as a v-only supply.
- T-100c THE RESIDUAL'S DIAGONAL DOES NOT CONVERT EITHER (operator ON, mix 0.5): diag of the rank-k residual as a v-only supply, all layers: r35 k=2 6.72e-8, k=16 3.54e-8; r100 k=2 3.49e-8, k=16 2.65e-8; layers 9-15 only, k=16: r35 2.92e-8 (+8%), r100 2.68e-8 (-4%) - while the half-TRUTH v at layers 9-15 gives r35 2.153e-8 (-20%), r100 1.961e-8 (-29%). Per-unit corr 0.86-0.90 with dv (the rank-16 diagonal) is not sufficient; the v channel wants the exact per-unit field (T-80e's structure condition), and Keenan's converting write was a TRAINED compensator (end-to-end through a differentiable recursion), not the truth residual. Late-layer half-truth v alone is the -20/-29% reference for any v supply.
- T-100d NO ZERO-COST WRITE DIRECTION (8 panel nets, layers 10-14): the covariance residual projected on computable directions - mu-hat mu-hat^T captures 13-30% of |E|^2 with a per-net coefficient 0.10-0.23 (not universal), gate*mu 3-9%, the card covariance's top eigenvector 6-26%; a 3-direction least-squares fit captures 15-34%. The residual's top mode is 0.8-aligned with the mean but is not the mean; a universal-coefficient write does not exist. Keenan's route (a trained rank-16 per-neuron-factor write, end-to-end through a differentiable recursion, co-adapted) is the only measured way such a write converts: for us it requires the torch shim of the card (G22 section 5 estimate: 1-2 days) and the 688-net all-layer truths (exist).
- T-101 NOISE TOLERANCE OF THE LATE-LAYER v CHANNEL (operator ON, half v at layers 9-15): supply from the 32M truth r35 2.153e-8 (-20%) / r100 1.961e-8 (-29%); from the 8M truth (per-unit v noise ~0.05% vs dv 0.1-0.3%, i.e. ~3-10% noise energy at these layers) r35 2.208e-8 (-18%) / r100 2.122e-8 (-24%). A supply with per-unit corr ~0.97 keeps ~85% of the value; the channel needs the exact per-unit field's DIRECTION (T-100c) but tolerates a few percent of incoherent noise energy. Graph reconciled to V45 (reconcile_20260903_late_layer_v45.py, validated); memory p2-late-born-error-and-lowrank-residual written.
- T-102 PLAN (the one measured converting mechanism for the v channel): Keenan's trained compensator = a rank-r per-neuron-factor write into the covariance state at layers 9-15, trained END-TO-END through the recursion on the 688 nets' all-layer post-mean truths (the G22 rows exist; the operator stays as the final-patch, co-trained). Requirements: (1) a torch shim of the card's layer loop (fnp -> torch; ~40 ops, 112 in-place sites; the CP3 Winograd arena as plain blocked GEMMs; float32 on MPS; checkpointing across 15 layers, RSS 5-8 GB) with a bit-level certificate vs estimator_retro at full precision; (2) the write module: u_k[i] = MLP(6 standardized per-unit scalars: m, v, k3, k4, gate, alpha) x latent, r = 16, scaled by sigma_i, layers 9-15 (T-98), warm-started at zero (a no-op); (3) loss = the F2J all-layer post-mean loss with the final layer re-weighted; batch 3 nets, 6 epochs; 4-fold CV on the 688 rows; (4) grade with the operator on the independent 4M truths (r35/r100) and the 8-net panel. Prize bracket: the late-layer half-v reference -20/-29% raw at util 0.61 -> ~1.3e-8 adjusted; the exact-late-states ceiling 1.2e-8 raw (chain host). Not the 4e-9 class alone (needs the cheaper host too). Effort: shim 1-2 days, training hours.
- T-103 MEAN-LEAK ORACLE (RETRO_MEANLEAK_TRUTH: the true post-mean error delta fed into the recursion; independent 4M; operator OFF numbers): the post covariance is C = M11 - m m^T, so a per-unit post-mean error leaks into it as -(m d^T + d m^T) - d d^T: rank 2, paired +/- spectrum, mean-aligned = the T-100 residual's signature. Covariance-only correction diverges (3.3e-6; r100 1e2); with the corrected mean ALSO carried forward: r35 8.62e-8 -> 2.13e-8 (-75%), r100 7.70e-8 -> 2.37e-8 (-69%) with NO operator; with the deployed operator on top it double-counts (3.8e-8 / 6.0e-8). The operator estimates exactly this delta per layer (64.5% held-out) but is only patched onto the output rows: the recursion never sees it ([instruction removed]).
- T-104 CLOSED LOOP WITH THE UNTRAINED OPERATOR DIVERGES (RETRO_CLOSED_LOOP=1: _cl_step = transport_correction stepped online; its correction carried into mean + consistent covariance): r35 4.25e-7, r100 6.41e-7, mean-only 6.45e-7, mix 0.5 8.72e-8 (= host). The operator was trained open-loop on the uncorrected host's tape; fed back it over-corrects and compounds. Keenan's fix: train through the recursion. Cheap alternatives: a late-window closed loop, damping, or the ITERATED open-loop retrain (closed-loop rows -> refit -> repeat) with the existing rows/refit machinery.

PLAN T-105 (teacher forcing at the HOST level, Keenan's mechanism without backprop): rows from the oracle recursion (true post means fed back with the consistent covariance; the row's host = the ONE-STEP mean before the correction; targets = the organizers' 1e9 all-layer means, which exist for the 688 nets incl. ranks 35-234) -> refit the operator on those rows (frozen 160/40 protocol) -> export the payload -> closed-loop inference (its own corrections fed back). The one-step (freshly born) error at the last layers is ~2.2e-8: the closed-loop route's ceiling is its predictability from the tape. 200 rows generating (29 s each); then the frozen-protocol refit on (one-step host, oracle tape) = the teacher-forced operator; then closed-loop inference with its own corrections fed back.

- T-108 FAILURE-ANALYSIS PROTOCOL FOR THE CLOSED LOOP ([instruction removed]). Pre-registered diagnostics, all with existing instruments: (1) DRIFT PROFILE: per-layer one-step error under operator_k's own feedback (rows_closedloop host.npy) vs the oracle's (rows_meanleak) -> early- or late-born mismatch. (2) PER-LAYER REMOVAL in closed loop: one-step error before vs after the correction at each layer (rows = pre vs the carried means) -> which layers/units the operator fails on (split units by firing rate, sigma, |m|). (3) TEACHER-FORCING LADDER: oracle feedback at layers <= L0, operator feedback after (RETRO_MEANLEAK_LAYERS=1-L0 + RETRO_CLOSED_LOOP_LAYERS=(L0+1)-15) -> where compounding starts and how much of the final error is accumulated vs freshly born. (4) MEAN vs COVARIANCE drift: oracle covariance feedback with operator means (and the converse) -> which state carries the exposure gap. (5) ITERATION DYNAMICS: rows_closedloop one-step errors per iteration -> contraction or oscillation. FIXES mapped to causes: early drift -> exposure-mixed rows (blend oracle/own feedback); late removal cap -> add the operator's own cumulative correction (the closed-loop state) and late-layer mechanistic features (G28 dk3 R^2 0.6) as inputs; oscillation -> payload averaging / damped iteration; covariance-carried drift -> a learned covariance write (Keenan's rank-16); all else -> the differentiable card.
- T-108a TEACHER-FORCING LADDER, operator_1 (r35, F2J-off row): oracle means fed back through L4 then own feedback -> 3.41e-8; through L8 -> 3.15e-8; through L12 -> 2.31e-8; through L14 (teacher-forced) -> 6.6e-9; none (pure closed loop) -> 3.28e-8. The exposure gap is BORN IN THE LAST THREE LAYERS: with exact upstream through L12, operator_1's own corrections at L13-14 leave residuals that derail L15 (2.31e-8 vs 6.6e-9). Sub-problem = the 40% one-step residual at L13-14 propagating into L15; iteration 2 trains exactly on that distribution; if it does not close, the cause is the per-layer removal cap at L13-15 -> specialize/enrich the operator there (mechanistic late-layer inputs, a second late-layer pass, its own cumulative correction as input).
- T-108b PER-LAYER REMOVAL IN THE CLOSED LOOP (closedloop_layers.py, r35, operator_1): one-step error -> after its correction: L4 5.8e-9 -> 4.1e-9 (29%), L8 2.07e-8 -> 1.47e-8 (29%), L12 4.56e-8 -> 3.27e-8 (28%), L15 4.51e-8 -> 3.16e-8 (30%) - the operator removes ~30% at EVERY layer in its own loop vs 60% teacher-forced: off-distribution inputs (drifted tape + recurrent state) halve its effectiveness uniformly, and the uncorrected 70% drives the one-step errors to 2x the oracle's. Iteration 2 trains on this distribution (the cure for the input mismatch); the residual question is the in-distribution removal fraction. VIRGIN REFERENCES (deployed card, score_candidate.py, 4M truths): 3016500 1.89e-8, ..501 2.37e-8, ..502 4.10e-8, ..503 2.40e-8, ..504 2.45e-8, ..505 3.07e-8; mean raw 2.712e-8, util 0.608, adjusted 1.645e-8.
- T-109 ITERATION 2 DIVERGED (REFIT_MEANLEAK_IT2: rows = operator_1's closed loop): valid-40 3.37e-8 vs its one-step host 5.01e-8 = 32.9% removal (60% on the oracle upstream); closed loop with operator_2: r35 5.82e-8, r100 1.05e-7 (operator_1: 3.28e-8 / 3.73e-8). CAUSE (T-108 diagnostics 1-3): in the loop the one-step error = fresh part (60% predictable) + the transported residual the operator missed upstream, and that residual is unobservable from the tape (removal 30% at every layer in-loop, T-108b; the ladder puts the gap in the last three layers, T-108a); pure DAgger makes each round's target less predictable -> divergence. ATTACK: scheduled sampling - rows with the fed-back correction = alpha x true delta + (1-alpha) x operator delta (RETRO_CLOSED_LOOP_EXPOSURE), alpha 0.5 first, then decreasing; the operator learns on intermediate distributions instead of the worst one.
- T-109b SCHEDULED-SAMPLING ROWS ARE SANE (r35, alpha 0.5 truth share, operator_1): one-step error L4 4.4e-9 / L8 1.42e-8 / L12 2.46e-8 / L15 2.47e-8 - between the oracle (4.1e-9 / 1.33e-8 / 2.31e-8 / 2.25e-8) and the pure loop (5.8e-9 / 2.07e-8 / 4.56e-8 / 4.51e-8), close to the oracle. (An earlier divergence was the oracle hook firing alongside the blend; RETRO_MEANLEAK_OFF=1 now guards it.) COVARIANCE-CONSISTENCY LAW (T-108 diagnostic 4): operator means with a TRUTH-consistent covariance diverge (2.9e-4): the covariance update must use exactly the delta carried in the mean; consistent updates with an imperfect delta are stable, inconsistent ones explode - the exposure gap cannot be split by channel. rows_mixed generating (two drivers).
- T-110 THE 30% DECOMPOSED: teacher-forced per-layer removal of operator_1 is 24% (L2) -> 60% (L8-15) (REFIT_MEANLEAK_RESULT all-200 layer MSEs vs the oracle one-step host); in its own loop the one-step error = fresh part (~oracle level) + the TRANSPORTED residual of what it missed upstream (about equal at depth), and it removes ~60% of the fresh part and ~0% of the transported part -> 30% overall, exactly as observed (T-108b). Without its recurrent state the loop is worse (6.2e-8 vs 3.28e-8), so the state helps; the wall is the transported residual, which by construction is absent from the oracle rows and unpredictable from local tape in the loop rows (why DAgger diverged, T-109). CHAIN OF IDEAS: the OPEN-loop operator's whole skill is transporting an accumulated error estimate (65% at the final layer). Two-stage design: closed-loop operator_1 corrects the fresh births in the recursion; a second, open-loop operator refit on the closed-loop host's CARRIED means (targets = its accumulated residual) patches the output rows. Testing: (a) the deployed open-loop patch on top of the closed loop as-is; (b) rows_clcarried -> refit -> patch.

With 97% teacher-forced interior accuracy the loop's error is off-distribution inputs (drifted host tape + mechanistic features), not unpredictability -> scheduled sampling for the GNN (rows from its own loop blended with truth, alpha 0.5, mech15 features; fine-tune), plus the final-layer features.

- T-115 DEFINITIVE LEARNED CLOSURE (GNN_M15_C64_E40: 160/40 protocol split, 200 rows with final-layer features, 64 channels, 40 epochs): valid-40 final 6.40e-9 (71.4% removal), interior L8/12/14 99/99/99%. The final layer barely moved with its 19 features (70.1 -> 71.4%): a CLIFF between the interior (99%) and the final read (71%) - the final layer's one-step error has a ~29% component the features do not carry (the final read uses a different mean path: the diagonal reads + _univariate_edgeworth, no pair map). CLOSED LOOP: r35 8.15e-8, r100 6.54e-8 - worse than the partial model (5.6e-8 / 4.0e-8): a more accurate in-distribution model is more brittle off-distribution.
